</button>\n      <button onclick=\"switchTab('ledger')\" class=\"nav-btn flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 flex-1 text-on-surface-variant\" data-tab=\"ledger\">\n        <span Bridge Integration -->\n  <script>\n    let activeTab = 'dashboard';\n    let currentPaperWidth = 80;\n    let currentLastSlipNo = 'GP-9485';\n    let cachedProducts = [];\n    let cachedParties = [];\n    let cachedTransactions = [];\n    let posCart = {}; // productId -> count\n    let ledgerFilter = 'ALL';\n\n    function bridge() {\n      return window.AndroidBridge || null;\n    }\n\n    function vibrate(ms = 30) {\n      if (bridge() && bridge().vibrate) {\n        bridge().vibrate(ms);\n      } else if (navigator.vibrate) {\n        navigator.vibrate(ms);\n      }\n    }\n\n    function showToast(msg) {\n      if (bridge() && bridge().showToast) {\n        bridge().showToast(msg);\n      } else {\n        console.log(msg);\n      }\n    }\n\n    function switchTab(tabId) {\n      vibrate(25);\n      activeTab = tabId;\n\n      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-page').forEach(page => {\n        page.classList.add('hidden');\n      });\n      const targetPage = document.getElementById('tab-' + tabId);\n      if (targetPage) targetPage.classList.remove('hidden');\n\n      document.querySelectorAll('.nav-btn').forEach(btn => {\n        const isSelected = btn.getAttribute('data-tab') === tabId;\n        btn.className = isSelected\n          ? 'nav-btn flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 flex-1 text-primary'\n          : 'nav-btn flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 flex-1 text-on-surface-variant';\n      });\n\n      // Show/hide POS cart bottom bar\n      const cartBar = document.getElementById('posCartBottomBar');\n      if (tabId === 'pos' && getTotalCartBags() > 0) {\n        cartBar.classList.remove('hidden');\n      } else {\n        cartBar.classList.add('hidden');\n      }\n\n      if (tabId === 'thermal') {\n        loadSlipPreview(currentLastSlipNo);\n      } else if (tabId === 'ledger') {\n        renderLedger();\n      }\n    }\n\n    function refreshData() {\n      vibrate(30);\n      try {\n        if (bridge()) {\n          const telemetryStr = bridge().getDashboardTelemetry();\n          const productsStr = bridge().getProducts();\n          const partiesStr = bridge().getParties();\n          const txStr = bridge().getTransactions();\n          currentLastSlipNo = bridge().getLastSlipNo() || 'GP-9485';\n\n          if (telemetryStr) updateDashboardTelemetry(JSON.parse(telemetryStr));\n          if (productsStr) {\n            cachedProducts = JSON.parse(productsStr);\n            renderPosProducts();\n            populateProductDropdowns();\n          }\n          if (partiesStr) {\n            cachedParties = JSON.parse(partiesStr);\n            populatePartyDropdowns();\n          }\n          if (txStr) {\n            cachedTransactions = JSON.parse(txStr);\n            renderDashboardActivities();\n            renderLedger();\n          }\n        }\n      } catch (e) {\n        console.error(\"Error refreshing data: \", e);\n      }\n    }\n\n    function updateDashboardTelemetry(t) {\n      if (!t) return;\n      document.getElementById('metricTotalBags').textContent = Number(t.totalStoredBags || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN');\n      document.getElementById('metricNetMt').textContent = `Net: ${(t.totalMetricTons || 0).toFixed(2)} MT`;\n      document.getElementById('metricInwardBags').textContent = Number(t.inwardDayBags || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN');\n      document.getElementById('metricDispatchedBags').textContent = Number(t.dispatchedBags || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN');\n      document.getElementById('metricPendingSlips').textContent = t.pendingSlipsCount || 0;\n      document.getElementById('dashLastSlipNo').textContent = currentLastSlipNo;\n\n      if (t.brandDistribution && Array.isArray(t.brandDistribution)) {\n        const bar = document.getElementById('brandDistributionBar');\n        const labels = document.getElementById('brandLabels');\n        bar.innerHTML = '';\n        labels.innerHTML = '';\n\n        const colors = ['#964900', '#506169', '#f57c00', '#1b6d24', '#ba1a1a'];\n        t.brandDistribution.forEach((b, idx) => {\n          const c = colors[idx % colors.length];\n          const seg = document.createElement('div');\n          seg.style.width = Math.max(b.percentage, 2) + '%';\n          seg.style.backgroundColor = c;\n          seg.title = `${b.brandName}: ${b.percentage.toFixed(1)}%`;\n          bar.appendChild(seg);\n\n          const lbl = document.createElement('div');\n          lbl.className = 'flex items-center gap-1 truncate';\n          lbl.innerHTML = `<span class=\"w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0\" style=\"background-color: ${c}\"></span><span class=\"truncate font-semibold\">${b.brandName}: ${b.bags}</span>`;\n          labels.appendChild(lbl);\n        });\n      }\n    }\n\n    function renderDashboardActivities() {\n      const list = document.getElementById('dashActivityList');\n      if (!list) return;\n      list.innerHTML = '';\n      const recent = cachedTransactions.slice(0, 4);\n\n      if (recent.length === 0) {\n        list.innerHTML = '<div class=\"text-[12px] text-on-surface-variant text-center py-2\">No recent activity</div>';\n        return;\n      }\n\n      recent.forEach(tx => {\n        const isOut = tx.type === 'OUTWARD';\n        const card = document.createElement('div');\n        card.className = 'bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-xl p-2.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 shadow-sm border border-surface-container-high cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99]';\n        card.onclick = () => viewSlip(tx.slipNo);\n        card.innerHTML = `\n          <div class=\"flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0\">\n            <div class=\"w-9 h-9 rounded-lg ${isOut ? 'bg-primary-fixed text-primary' : 'bg-tertiary-fixed text-tertiary'} flex items-center justify-center shrink-0\">\n              <span class=\"material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]\">${isOut ? 'local_shipping' : 'archive'}</span>\n            </div>\n            <div class=\"flex flex-col min-w-0\">\n              <div class=\"flex items-center gap-1.5\">\n                <span class=\"font-mono text-[12px] font-bold text-on-surface\">${tx.slipNo}</span>\n                <span class=\"text-[9px] font-bold uppercase px-1 rounded ${isOut ? 'bg-primary-fixed text-primary' : 'bg-tertiary-fixed text-tertiary'}\">${isOut ? 'Dispatched' : 'Stock-In'}</span>\n              </div>\n              <span class=\"text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant truncate\">${tx.totalBags} Bags • ${tx.partyName}</span>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n          <div class=\"text-right shrink-0\">\n            <span class=\"text-[10px] text-on-surface-variant\">${tx.timeStr}</span>\n            <span class=\"block text-[11px] font-bold text-primary\">${tx.totalMetricTons.toFixed(2)} MT</span>\n          </div>\n        `;\n        list.appendChild(card);\n      });\n    }\n\n    function renderPosProducts(filter = 'all') {\n      const grid = document.getElementById('posProductsGrid');\n      if (!grid) return;\n      grid.innerHTML = '';\n\n      cachedProducts.forEach(p => {\n        if (filter !== 'all' && p.category !== filter) return;\n\n        const inCart = posCart[p.id] || 0;\n        const tile = document.createElement('div');\n        tile.className = 'bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-xl p-3 border border-surface-container-high shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between select-none';\n        tile.innerHTML = `\n          <div>\n            <div class=\"flex items-start justify-between\">\n              <div class=\"w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-primary/10 text-primary flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-[13px]\">\n                ${p.brandName.substring(0, 2).toUpperCase()}\n              </div>\n              <span class=\"${inCart > 0 ? '' : 'hidden'} px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[9px] font-bold bg-primary-container text-on-primary\" id=\"posBadge-${p.id}\">${inCart} in cart</span>\n            </div>\n            <div class=\"mt-1.5\">\n              <div class=\"flex items-center gap-1\">\n                <span class=\"text-[9px] px-1 py-0.2 bg-tertiary-fixed text-on-tertiary-fixed rounded font-bold uppercase\">${p.grade}</span>\n                <span class=\"font-mono text-[9px] text-on-surface-variant\">${p.bayLocation}</span>\n              </div>\n              <h4 class=\"text-[13px] font-bold text-on-surface mt-1 line-clamp-1 leading-tight\">${p.name}</h4>\n              <div class=\"flex items-baseline justify-between mt-1\">\n                <span class=\"text-[16px] font-extrabold text-primary\">₹${p.defaultRatePerBag}<span class=\"text-[10px] font-normal text-on-surface-variant\">/bag</span></span>\n                <span class=\"font-mono text-[10px] text-on-surface-variant\">${p.currentStockBags} Stock</span>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n          <div class=\"mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-surface-container grid grid-cols-3 gap-1\">\n            <button onclick=\"addPosItem('${p.id}', 1)\" class=\"h-8 rounded bg-surface-container text-on-surface text-[11px] font-bold active:scale-90 transition-transform flex items-center justify-center\">+1</button>\n            <button onclick=\"addPosItem('${p.id}', 10)\" class=\"h-8 rounded bg-surface-container text-on-surface text-[11px] font-bold active:scale-90 transition-transform flex items-center justify-center\">+10</button>\n            <button onclick=\"addPosItem('${p.id}', 50)\" class=\"h-8 rounded bg-primary-fixed text-on-primary-fixed text-[11px] font-bold active:scale-90 transition-transform flex items-center justify-center\">+50</button>\n          </div>\n        `;\n        grid.appendChild(tile);\n      });\n    }\n\n    function filterPosCategory(cat, btn) {\n      vibrate(20);\n      document.querySelectorAll('.cat-pill').forEach(el => {\n        el.className = 'cat-pill px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold uppercase shrink-0 bg-surface-container-lowest text-on-surface-variant border border-surface-container-high';\n      });\n      btn.className = 'cat-pill px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold uppercase shrink-0 bg-primary-container text-on-primary shadow-sm';\n      renderPosProducts(cat);\n    }\n\n    function addPosItem(productId, delta) {\n      vibrate(35);\n      const cur = posCart[productId] || 0;\n      const product = cachedProducts.find(p => p.id === productId);\n      if (!product) return;\n\n      const next = Math.max(0, cur + delta);\n      if (next > product.currentStockBags) {\n        showToast(`Cannot add: only ${product.currentStockBags} bags in stock`);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (next === 0) {\n        delete posCart[productId];\n      } else {\n        posCart[productId] = next;\n      }\n\n      // Update badge\n      const badge = document.getElementById(`posBadge-${productId}`);\n      if (badge) {\n        if (next > 0) {\n          badge.textContent = `${next} in cart`;\n          badge.classList.remove('hidden');\n        } else {\n          badge.classList.add('hidden');\n        }\n      }\n\n      updatePosCartBar();\n    }\n\n    function getTotalCartBags() {\n      return Object.values(posCart).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);\n    }\n\n    function updatePosCartBar() {\n      const totalBags = getTotalCartBags();\n      let totalAmount = 0;\n      let totalMt = 0;\n\n      for (const [pId, qty] of Object.entries(posCart)) {\n        const p = cachedProducts.find(x => x.id === pId);\n        if (p) {\n          totalAmount += qty * p.defaultRatePerBag;\n          totalMt += (qty * p.weightPerBagKg) / 1000.0;\n        }\n      }\n\n      document.getElementById('posCartTotalBags').textContent = totalBags;\n      document.getElementById('posCartTotalMt').textContent = totalMt.toFixed(2);\n      document.getElementById('posCartTotalAmount').textContent = totalAmount.toLocaleString('en-IN');\n\n      const bar = document.getElementById('posCartBottomBar');\n      if (activeTab === 'pos' && totalBags > 0) {\n        bar.classList.remove('hidden');\n      } else {\n        bar.classList.add('hidden');\n      }\n    }\n\n    function checkoutPosCart() {\n      vibrate(40);\n      const totalBags = getTotalCartBags();\n      if (totalBags === 0) {\n        showToast(\"Cart is empty\");\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const partySelect = document.getElementById('posPartySelect');\n      const partyName = partySelect ? partySelect.value : \"Direct Walk-in Contractor\";\n\n      // If only one product in cart, use quick dispatch\n      const keys = Object.keys(posCart);\n      if (keys.length === 1) {\n        const pId = keys[0];\n        const qty = posCart[pId];\n        if (bridge()) {\n          const resStr = bridge().recordQuickDispatch(pId, qty, partyName);\n          const res = JSON.parse(resStr);\n          if (res.success) {\n            currentLastSlipNo = res.slipNo;\n            posCart = {};\n            updatePosCartBar();\n            refreshData();\n            switchTab('thermal');\n            showToast(`Slip ${res.slipNo} issued!`);\n          } else {\n            alert(res.error || \"Dispatch failed\");\n          }\n        }\n      } else {\n        // Multi-item dispatch\n        const items = [];\n        for (const [pId, qty] of Object.entries(posCart)) {\n          items.push({ productId: pId, bags: qty });\n        }\n        if (bridge()) {\n          const resStr = bridge().recordDispatch(\n            partyName,\n            \"MH-12-QZ-4891\",\n            \"Express Driver\",\n            \"+91 98220-44102\",\n            \"Express POS Counter\",\n            \"DC-POS-\" + (Date.now() % 10000),\n            \"-\",\n            JSON.stringify(items)\n          );\n          const res = JSON.parse(resStr);\n          if (res.success) {\n            currentLastSlipNo = res.slipNo;\n            posCart = {};\n            updatePosCartBar();\n            refreshData();\n            switchTab('thermal');\n            showToast(`Gate Pass ${res.slipNo} issued!`);\n          } else {\n            alert(res.error || \"Dispatch failed\");\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    function populatePartyDropdowns() {\n      const posSelect = document.getElementById('posPartySelect');\n      const dispatchSelect = document.getElementById('dispatchPartySelect');\n      if (!posSelect || !dispatchSelect) return;\n\n      posSelect.innerHTML = '';\n      dispatchSelect.innerHTML = '';\n\n      cachedParties.forEach(p => {\n        const opt = document.createElement('option');\n        opt.value = p.name;\n        opt.textContent = `${p.name} [${p.accountNo}]`;\n        posSelect.appendChild(opt.cloneNode(true));\n        dispatchSelect.appendChild(opt);\n      });\n\n      const walkIn = document.createElement('option');\n      walkIn.value = 'Direct Walk-in Contractor';\n      walkIn.textContent = 'Direct Walk-in Contractor [CASH/UPI]';\n      posSelect.appendChild(walkIn.cloneNode(true));\n      dispatchSelect.appendChild(walkIn);\n    }\n\n    function populateProductDropdowns() {\n      const dSelect = document.getElementById('dispatchProductSelect');\n      const sSelect = document.getElementById('stockInProductSelect');\n      if (!dSelect || !sSelect) return;\n\n      dSelect.innerHTML = '';\n      sSelect.innerHTML = '';\n\n      cachedProducts.forEach(p => {\n        const opt = document.createElement('option');\n        opt.value = p.id;\n        opt.textContent = `${p.name} (${p.currentStockBags} bags available)`;\n        dSelect.appendChild(opt.cloneNode(true));\n\n        const opt2 = document.createElement('option');\n        opt2.value = p.id;\n        opt2.textContent = `${p.name} (${p.bayLocation})`;\n        sSelect.appendChild(opt2);\n      });\n    }\n\n    function submitFullDispatch() {\n      vibrate(40);\n      const party = document.getElementById('dispatchPartySelect').value;\n      const vehicle = document.getElementById('dispatchVehicle').value.trim();\n      const site = document.getElementById('dispatchSite').value.trim();\n      const driver = document.getElementById('dispatchDriver').value.trim();\n      const phone = document.getElementById('dispatchPhone').value.trim();\n      const challan = document.getElementById('dispatchChallan').value.trim();\n      const ewb = document.getElementById('dispatchEwb').value.trim();\n      const pId = document.getElementById('dispatchProductSelect').value;\n      const bags = parseInt(document.getElementById('dispatchBagsInput').value, 10) || 0;\n\n      if (bags <= 0) {\n        alert(\"Please enter a valid quantity of bags\");\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const items = [{ productId: pId, bags: bags }];\n      if (bridge()) {\n        const resStr = bridge().recordDispatch(party, vehicle, driver, phone, site, challan, ewb, JSON.stringify(items));\n        const res = JSON.parse(resStr);\n        if (res.success) {\n          currentLastSlipNo = res.slipNo;\n          refreshData();\n          switchTab('thermal');\n          showToast(`Dispatch Pass ${res.slipNo} Created!`);\n        } else {\n          alert(res.error || \"Dispatch failed\");\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    function openStockInModal() {\n      vibrate(25);\n      document.getElementById('stockInModal').classList.remove('hidden');\n    }\n\n    function closeStockInModal() {\n      vibrate(25);\n      document.getElementById('stockInModal').classList.add('hidden');\n    }\n\n    function submitStockIn() {\n      vibrate(40);\n      const pId = document.getElementById('stockInProductSelect').value;\n      const bags = parseInt(document.getElementById('stockInBags').value, 10) || 0;\n      const bay = document.getElementById('stockInBay').value.trim();\n      const batch = document.getElementById('stockInBatch').value.trim();\n\n      if (bags <= 0) {\n        alert(\"Stock in bags must be greater than zero\");\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (bridge()) {\n        const resStr = bridge().recordStockIn(pId, bags, batch, bay);\n        const res = JSON.parse(resStr);\n        if (res.success) {\n          closeStockInModal();\n          refreshData();\n          showToast(`Stock In +${bags} bags recorded!`);\n        } else {\n          alert(res.error || \"Stock in failed\");\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    function loadSlipPreview(slipNo) {\n      if (bridge()) {\n        const text = bridge().getSlipText(slipNo || currentLastSlipNo, currentPaperWidth);\n </button>\n      <button onclick=\"switchTab('ledger')\" class=\"nav-btn flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 flex-1 text-on-surface-variant\" data-tab=\"ledger\">\n        <span Bridge Integration -->\n  <script>\n    let activeTab = 'dashboard';\n    let currentPaperWidth = 80;\n    let currentLastSlipNo = 'GP-9485';\n    let cachedProducts = [];\n    let cachedParties = [];\n    let cachedTransactions = [];\n    let posCart = {}; // productId -> count\n    let ledgerFilter = 'ALL';\n\n    function bridge() {\n      return window.AndroidBridge || null;\n    }\n\n    function vibrate(ms = 30) {\n      if (bridge() && bridge().vibrate) {\n        bridge().vibrate(ms);\n      } else if (navigator.vibrate) {\n        navigator.vibrate(ms);\n      }\n    }\n\n    function showToast(msg) {\n      if (bridge() && bridge().showToast) {\n        bridge().showToast(msg);\n      } else {\n        console.log(msg);\n      }\n    }\n\n    function switchTab(tabId) {\n      vibrate(25);\n      activeTab = tabId;\n\n      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-page').forEach(page => {\n        page.classList.add('hidden');\n      });\n      const targetPage = document.getElementById('tab-' + tabId);\n      if (targetPage) targetPage.classList.remove('hidden');\n\n      document.querySelectorAll('.nav-btn').forEach(btn => {\n        const isSelected = btn.getAttribute('data-tab') === tabId;\n        btn.className = isSelected\n          ? 'nav-btn flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 flex-1 text-primary'\n          : 'nav-btn flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 flex-1 text-on-surface-variant';\n      });\n\n      // Show/hide POS cart bottom bar\n      const cartBar = document.getElementById('posCartBottomBar');\n      if (tabId === 'pos' && getTotalCartBags() > 0) {\n        cartBar.classList.remove('hidden');\n      } else {\n        cartBar.classList.add('hidden');\n      }\n\n      if (tabId === 'thermal') {\n        loadSlipPreview(currentLastSlipNo);\n      } else if (tabId === 'ledger') {\n        renderLedger();\n      }\n    }\n\n    function refreshData() {\n      vibrate(30);\n      try {\n        if (bridge()) {\n          const telemetryStr = bridge().getDashboardTelemetry();\n          const productsStr = bridge().getProducts();\n          const partiesStr = bridge().getParties();\n          const txStr = bridge().getTransactions();\n          currentLastSlipNo = bridge().getLastSlipNo() || 'GP-9485';\n\n          if (telemetryStr) updateDashboardTelemetry(JSON.parse(telemetryStr));\n          if (productsStr) {\n            cachedProducts = JSON.parse(productsStr);\n            renderPosProducts();\n            populateProductDropdowns();\n          }\n          if (partiesStr) {\n            cachedParties = JSON.parse(partiesStr);\n            populatePartyDropdowns();\n          }\n          if (txStr) {\n            cachedTransactions = JSON.parse(txStr);\n            renderDashboardActivities();\n            renderLedger();\n          }\n        }\n      } catch (e) {\n        console.error(\"Error refreshing data: \", e);\n      }\n    }\n\n    function updateDashboardTelemetry(t) {\n      if (!t) return;\n      document.getElementById('metricTotalBags').textContent = Number(t.totalStoredBags || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN');\n      document.getElementById('metricNetMt').textContent = `Net: ${(t.totalMetricTons || 0).toFixed(2)} MT`;\n      document.getElementById('metricInwardBags').textContent = Number(t.inwardDayBags || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN');\n      document.getElementById('metricDispatchedBags').textContent = Number(t.dispatchedBags || 0).toLocaleString('en-IN');\n      document.getElementById('metricPendingSlips').textContent = t.pendingSlipsCount || 0;\n      document.getElementById('dashLastSlipNo').textContent = currentLastSlipNo;\n\n      if (t.brandDistribution && Array.isArray(t.brandDistribution)) {\n        const bar = document.getElementById('brandDistributionBar');\n        const labels = document.getElementById('brandLabels');\n        bar.innerHTML = '';\n        labels.innerHTML = '';\n\n        const colors = ['#964900', '#506169', '#f57c00', '#1b6d24', '#ba1a1a'];\n        t.brandDistribution.forEach((b, idx) => {\n          const c = colors[idx % colors.length];\n          const seg = document.createElement('div');\n          seg.style.width = Math.max(b.percentage, 2) + '%';\n          seg.style.backgroundColor = c;\n          seg.title = `${b.brandName}: ${b.percentage.toFixed(1)}%`;\n          bar.appendChild(seg);\n\n          const lbl = document.createElement('div');\n          lbl.className = 'flex items-center gap-1 truncate';\n          lbl.innerHTML = `<span class=\"w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0\" style=\"background-color: ${c}\"></span><span class=\"truncate font-semibold\">${b.brandName}: ${b.bags}</span>`;\n          labels.appendChild(lbl);\n        });\n      }\n    }\n\n    function renderDashboardActivities() {\n      const list = document.getElementById('dashActivityList');\n      if (!list) return;\n      list.innerHTML = '';\n      const recent = cachedTransactions.slice(0, 4);\n\n      if (recent.length === 0) {\n        list.innerHTML = '<div class=\"text-[12px] text-on-surface-variant text-center py-2\">No recent activity</div>';\n        return;\n      }\n\n      recent.forEach(tx => {\n        const isOut = tx.type === 'OUTWARD';\n        const card = document.createElement('div');\n        card.className = 'bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-xl p-2.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 shadow-sm border border-surface-container-high cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99]';\n        card.onclick = () => viewSlip(tx.slipNo);\n        card.innerHTML = `\n          <div class=\"flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0\">\n            <div class=\"w-9 h-9 rounded-lg ${isOut ? 'bg-primary-fixed text-primary' : 'bg-tertiary-fixed text-tertiary'} flex items-center justify-center shrink-0\">\n              <span class=\"material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]\">${isOut ? 'local_shipping' : 'archive'}</span>\n            </div>\n            <div class=\"flex flex-col min-w-0\">\n              <div class=\"flex items-center gap-1.5\">\n                <span class=\"font-mono text-[12px] font-bold text-on-surface\">${tx.slipNo}</span>\n                <span class=\"text-[9px] font-bold uppercase px-1 rounded ${isOut ? 'bg-primary-fixed text-primary' : 'bg-tertiary-fixed text-tertiary'}\">${isOut ? 'Dispatched' : 'Stock-In'}</span>\n              </div>\n              <span class=\"text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant truncate\">${tx.totalBags} Bags • ${tx.partyName}</span>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n          <div class=\"text-right shrink-0\">\n            <span class=\"text-[10px] text-on-surface-variant\">${tx.timeStr}</span>\n            <span class=\"block text-[11px] font-bold text-primary\">${tx.totalMetricTons.toFixed(2)} MT</span>\n          </div>\n        `;\n        list.appendChild(card);\n      });\n    }\n\n    function renderPosProducts(filter = 'all') {\n      const grid = document.getElementById('posProductsGrid');\n      if (!grid) return;\n      grid.innerHTML = '';\n\n      cachedProducts.forEach(p => {\n        if (filter !== 'all' && p.category !== filter) return;\n\n        const inCart = posCart[p.id] || 0;\n        const tile = document.createElement('div');\n        tile.className = 'bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-xl p-3 border border-surface-container-high shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between select-none';\n        tile.innerHTML = `\n          <div>\n            <div class=\"flex items-start justify-between\">\n              <div class=\"w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-primary/10 text-primary flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-[13px]\">\n                ${p.brandName.substring(0, 2).toUpperCase()}\n              </div>\n              <span class=\"${inCart > 0 ? '' : 'hidden'} px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[9px] font-bold bg-primary-container text-on-primary\" id=\"posBadge-${p.id}\">${inCart} in cart</span>\n            </div>\n            <div class=\"mt-1.5\">\n              <div class=\"flex items-center gap-1\">\n                <span class=\"text-[9px] px-1 py-0.2 bg-tertiary-fixed text-on-tertiary-fixed rounded font-bold uppercase\">${p.grade}</span>\n                <span class=\"font-mono text-[9px] text-on-surface-variant\">${p.bayLocation}</span>\n              </div>\n              <h4 class=\"text-[13px] text-on-surface-variant\">${p.currentStockBags} Stock</span>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n          <div class=\"mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-surface-container grid items-center justify-center\">+1</button>\n            <button onclick=\"addPosItem('${p.id}', 10)\" class=\"h-8 rounded bg-surface-container text-on-surface text-[11px] font-bold active:scale-90 transition-transform flex items-center justify-center\">+10</button>\n            <button onclick=\"addPosItem('${p.id}', 50)\" class=\"h-8 rounded bg-primary-fixed text-on-primary-fixed text-[11px] font-bold active:scale-90 transition-transform flex items-center justify-center\">+50</button>\n          </div>\n        `;\n        grid.appendChild(tile);\n      });\n    }\n\n    function filterPosCategory(cat, btn) {\n      vibrate(20);\n      document.querySelectorAll('.cat-pill').forEach(el => {\n        el.className = 'cat-pill px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold uppercase shrink-0 bg-surface-container-lowest text-on-surface-variant border border-surface-container-high';\n      });\n      btn.className = 'cat-pill px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold uppercase shrink-0 bg-primary-container text-on-primary shadow-sm';\n      renderPosProducts(cat);\n    }\n\n    function addPosItem(productId, delta) {\n      vibrate(35);\n      const cur = posCart[productId] || 0;\n      const product = cachedProducts.find(p => p.id === productId);\n      if (!product) return;\n\n      const next = Math.max(0, cur + delta);\n      if (next > product.currentStockBags) {\n        showToast(`Cannot add: only ${product.currentStockBags} bags in stock`);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (next === 0) {\n        delete posCart[productId];\n      } else {\n        posCart[productId] = next;\n      }\n\n      // Update badge\n      const badge = document.getElementById(`posBadge-${productId}`);\n      if (badge) {\n        if (next > 0) {\n          badge.textContent = `${next} in cart`;\n          badge.classList.remove('hidden');\n        } else {\n          badge.classList.add('hidden');\n        }\n      }\n\n      updatePosCartBar();\n    }\n\n    function getTotalCartBags() {\n      return Object.values(posCart).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);\n    }\n\n    function updatePosCartBar() {\n      const totalBags = getTotalCartBags();\n      let totalAmount = 0;\n      let totalMt = 0;\n\n      for (const [pId, qty] of Object.entries(posCart)) {\n        const p = cachedProducts.find(x => x.id === pId);\n        if (p) {\n          totalAmount += qty * p.defaultRatePerBag;\n          totalMt += (qty * p.weightPerBagKg) / 1000.0;\n        }\n      }\n\n      document.getElementById('posCartTotalBags').textContent = totalBags;\n      document.getElementById('posCartTotalMt').textContent = totalMt.toFixed(2);\n      document.getElementById('posCartTotalAmount').textContent = totalAmount.toLocaleString('en-IN');\n\n      const bar = document.getElementById('posCartBottomBar');\n      if (activeTab === 'pos' && totalBags > 0) {\n        bar.classList.remove('hidden');\n      } else {\n        bar.classList.add('hidden');\n      }\n    }\n\n    function checkoutPosCart() {\n      vibrate(40);\n      const totalBags = getTotalCartBags();\n      if (totalBags === 0) {\n        showToast(\"Cart is empty\");\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const partySelect = document.getElementById('posPartySelect');\n      const partyName = partySelect ? partySelect.value : \"Direct Walk-in Contractor\";\n\n      // If only one product in cart, use quick dispatch\n      const keys = Object.keys(posCart);\n      if (keys.length === 1) {\n        const pId = keys[0];\n        const qty = posCart[pId];\n        if (bridge()) {\n          const resStr = bridge().recordQuickDispatch(pId, qty, partyName);\n          const res = JSON.parse(resStr);\n          if (res.success) {\n            currentLastSlipNo = res.slipNo;\n            posCart = {};\n            updatePosCartBar();\n            refreshData();\n            switchTab('thermal');\n            showToast(`Slip ${res.slipNo} issued!`);\n          } else {\n            alert(res.error || \"Dispatch failed\");\n          }\n        }\n      } else {\n        // Multi-item dispatch\n        const items = [];\n        for (const [pId, qty] of Object.entries(posCart)) {\n          items.push({ productId: pId, bags: qty });\n        }\n        if (bridge()) {\n          const resStr = bridge().recordDispatch(\n            partyName,\n            \"MH-12-QZ-4891\",\n            \"Express Driver\",\n            \"+91 98220-44102\",\n            \"Express POS Counter\",\n            \"DC-POS-\" + (Date.now() % 10000),\n            \"-\",\n            JSON.stringify(items)\n          );\n          const res = JSON.parse(resStr);\n          if (res.success) {\n            currentLastSlipNo = res.slipNo;\n            posCart = {};\n            updatePosCartBar();\n            refreshData();\n            switchTab('thermal');\n            showToast(`Gate Pass ${res.slipNo} issued!`);\n          } else {\n            alert(res.error || \"Dispatch failed\");\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    function populatePartyDropdowns() {\n      const posSelect = document.getElementById('posPartySelect');\n      const dispatchSelect = document.getElementById('dispatchPartySelect');\n      if (!posSelect || !dispatchSelect) return;\n\n      posSelect.innerHTML = '';\n      dispatchSelect.innerHTML = '';\n\n      cachedParties.forEach(p => {\n        const opt = document.createElement('option');\n        opt.value = p.name;\n        opt.textContent = `${p.name} [${p.accountNo}]`;\n        posSelect.appendChild(opt.cloneNode(true));\n        dispatchSelect.appendChild(opt);\n      });\n\n      const walkIn = document.createElement('option');\n      walkIn.value = 'Direct Walk-in Contractor';\n      walkIn.textContent = 'Direct Walk-in Contractor [CASH/UPI]';\n      posSelect.appendChild(walkIn.cloneNode(true));\n      dispatchSelect.appendChild(walkIn);\n    }\n\n    function populateProductDropdowns() {\n      const dSelect = document.getElementById('dispatchProductSelect');\n      const sSelect = document.getElementById('stockInProductSelect');\n      if (!dSelect || !sSelect) return;\n\n      dSelect.innerHTML = '';\n      sSelect.innerHTML = '';\n\n      cachedProducts.forEach(p => {\n        const opt = document.createElement('option');\n        opt.value = p.id;\n        opt.textContent = `${p.name} (${p.currentStockBags} bags available)`;\n        dSelect.appendChild(opt.cloneNode(true));\n\n        const opt2 = document.createElement('option');\n        opt2.value = p.id;\n        opt2.textContent = `${p.name} (${p.bayLocation})`;\n        sSelect.appendChild(opt2);\n      });\n    }\n\n    function submitFullDispatch() {\n      vibrate(40);\n      const party = document.getElementById('dispatchPartySelect').value;\n      const vehicle = document.getElementById('dispatchVehicle').value.trim();\n      const site = document.getElementById('dispatchSite').value.trim();\n      const driver = document.getElementById('dispatchDriver').value.trim();\n      const phone = document.getElementById('dispatchPhone').value.trim();\n      const challan = document.getElementById('dispatchChallan').value.trim();\n      const ewb = document.getElementById('dispatchEwb').value.trim();\n      const pId = document.getElementById('dispatchProductSelect').value;\n      const bags = parseInt(document.getElementById('dispatchBagsInput').value, 10) || 0;\n\n      if (bags <= 0) {\n        alert(\"Please enter a valid quantity of bags\");\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const items = [{ productId: pId, bags: bags }];\n      if (bridge()) {\n        const resStr = bridge().recordDispatch(party, vehicle, driver, phone, site, challan, ewb, JSON.stringify(items));\n        const res = JSON.parse(resStr);\n        if (res.success) {\n          currentLastSlipNo = res.slipNo;\n          refreshData();\n          switchTab('thermal');\n          showToast(`Dispatch Pass ${res.slipNo} Created!`);\n        } else {\n          alert(res.error || \"Dispatch failed\");\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    function openStockInModal() {\n      vibrate(25);\n      document.getElementById('stockInModal').classList.remove('hidden');\n    }\n\n    function closeStockInModal() {\n      vibrate(25);\n      document.getElementById('stockInModal').classList.add('hidden');\n    }\n\n    function submitStockIn() {\n      vibrate(40);\n      const pId = document.getElementById('stockInProductSelect').value;\n      const bags = parseInt(document.getElementById('stockInBags').value, 10) || 0;\n      const bay = document.getElementById('stockInBay').value.trim();\n      const batch = document.getElementById('stockInBatch').value.trim();\n\n      if (bags <= 0) {\n        alert(\"Stock in bags must be greater than zero\");\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (bridge()) {\n        const resStr = bridge().recordStockIn(pId, bags, batch, bay);\n        const res = JSON.parse(resStr);\n        if (res.success) {\n          closeStockInModal();\n          refreshData();\n          showToast(`Stock In +${bags} bags recorded!`);\n        } else {\n          alert(res.error || \"Stock in failed\");\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    function loadSlipPreview(slipNo) {\n      if (bridge()) {\n        const text = bridge().getSlipText(slipNo || currentLastSlipNo, currentPaperWidth);\n        document.getElementById('thermalSlipContent').textContent = text;\n      }\n    }\n\n    function setPaperWidth(width) {\n      vibrate(25);\n      currentPaperWidth = width;\n      const b58 = document.getElementById('btnPaper58');\n      const b80 = document.getElementById('btnPaper80');\n\n      if (width === 58) {\n        b58.className = 'flex-1 py-1 rounded-md text-center font-bold transition-all bg-surface-container-lowest text-primary shadow-sm';\n        b80.className = 'flex-1 py-1 rounded-md text-center font-bold transition-all text-on-surface-variant';\n      } else {\n        b80.className = 'flex-1 py-1 rounded-md text-center font-bold transition-all bg-surface-container-lowest text-primary shadow-sm';\n        b58.className = 'flex-1 py-1 rounded-md text-center font-bold transition-all text-on-surface-variant';\n      }\n\n      loadSlipPreview(currentLastSlipNo);\n    }\n\n    function triggerPrintSlip() {\n      vibrate(60);\n      const cutBar = document.getElementById('cutLineBar');\n      if (cutBar) {\n        cutBar.classList.remove('hidden');\n        setTimeout(() => cutBar.classList.add('hidden'), 1800);\n      }\n\n      if (bridge()) {\n        const resStr = bridge().printSlip(currentLastSlipNo, currentPaperWidth);\n        const res = JSON.parse(resStr);\n        if (!res.success) {\n          showToast(res.error || \"Print failed\");\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    function copySlipToClipboard() {\n      vibrate(25);\n      const text = document.getElementById('thermalSlipContent').textContent;\n      if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {\n        navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);\n        showToast(\"Slip copied to clipboard\");\n      } else {\n        showToast(\"Copied\");\n      }\n    }\n\n    function viewSlip(slipNo) {\n      currentLastSlipNo = slipNo;\n      switchTab('thermal');\n    }\n\n    function filterLedgerType(type, btn) {\n      vibrate(20);\n      ledgerFilter = type;\n      document.querySelectorAll('.ledger-pill').forEach(el => {\n        el.className = 'ledger-pill px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold uppercase bg-surface-container-lowest text-on-surface-variant border border-surface-container-high shrink-0';\n      });\n      btn.className = 'ledger-pill px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold
